The left encloses from the opposite side and bears a lexical association with failed fortune; the record itself is a binding decree and official enrollment, so handedness visibly assigns the recipient's place within it.","ayah_refs":["69:19","69:25"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Accounting becomes settlement of rights and wealth","parent_semantic_invariant":"Calculation issues in a binding settlement involving debt, obligation, property, authority, and the sufficiency or failure of accumulated means.","parent_surprising_reach":"The failed wealth scene reaches loan contracts, tribute, appraised reputation, marriage as sufficiency, land revenue, and a slave's staged payments toward release.","key":"A","name":"Decree, registration, debt, and completed payment","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A right is entered, adjudicated, and discharged through an enforceable record.","active_motifs":"إثبات يوجب حكما أو قدرا (`quranic:root_001283:B003/m01`); إدخال الاسم في سجل أو زمرة (`quranic:root_001283:B004/m01`); مكاتبة العبد على عتقه (`quranic:root_001283:B005/m01`); العد والحساب (`quranic:root_000318:B001/m01`); الحسبة والنظر في الأمر (`quranic:root_000318:B006/m01`); الحكم والأمر القاطع (`quranic:root_001237:B001/m01`); الإبلاغ والوصية القاطعة (`quranic:root_001237:B002/m01`); إحكام الصنع وإنفاذ الفعل (`quranic:root_001237:B005/m01`); أداء الحق واستيفاؤه (`quranic:root_001237:B006/m01`); المال المقدم (`quranic:root_000733:B005/m01`); الإتاوة المؤداة (`quranic:root_000009:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ)","synthesis":"The document fixes judgment, enrolls a name, or schedules emancipation payments. Counting and public oversight support adjudication; notification, crafted decree, debt settlement, an advance, and tribute complete the legal-economic circuit from entry to discharge.","ayah_refs":["69:19","69:25","69:20","69:26","69:27","69:24"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Accounting becomes settlement of rights and wealth","parent_semantic_invariant":"Calculation issues in a binding settlement involving debt, obligation, property, authority, and the sufficiency or failure of accumulated means.","parent_surprising_reach":"The failed wealth scene reaches loan contracts, tribute, appraised reputation, marriage as sufficiency, land revenue, and a slave's staged payments toward release.","key":"C","name":"Authority, proof, and sharp public power","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Rule depends on coercive capacity, a prevailing proof, delegated office, and a tongue able to impose its claim.","active_motifs":"السلاطة قوة قاهرة مسلطة (`quranic:root_000732:B001/m01`); السلطان حجة قاهرة (`quranic:root_000732:B002/m01`); السلطان قدرة وولاية (`quranic:root_000732:B003/m01`); لسان سليط حاد صخاب (`quranic:root_000732:B004/m01`); حدة وطول في الآلة والعضو (`quranic:root_000732:B006/m01`); القيل صاحب القول النافذ (`quranic:root_001272:B004/m01`); اقتالة الحكم على غيره (`quranic:root_001272:B010/m01`); الحكم والأمر القاطع (`quranic:root_001237:B001/m01`); الحسب والمآثر (`quranic:root_000318:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`س ل ط`: 69:29 (سُلْطَٰنِ); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ)","synthesis":"Coercion, demonstrative proof, delegated rule, sharp speech, and hard elongated instruments materialize 'authority.' The authoritative speaker and imposed decision show power becoming discourse, while inherited standing reveals the social capital that vanishes with the condemned person's dominion.","ayah_refs":["69:29","69:19","69:25","69:27","69:20","69:26"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Accounting becomes settlement of rights and wealth","parent_semantic_invariant":"Calculation issues in a binding settlement involving debt, obligation, property, authority, and the sufficiency or failure of accumulated means.","parent_surprising_reach":"The failed wealth scene reaches loan contracts, tribute, appraised reputation, marriage as sufficiency, land revenue, and a slave's staged payments toward release.","key":"D","name":"Marriage, alliance, and economic self-sufficiency","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Households join through contract and mutual relation, promising social and material sufficiency.","active_motifs":"مكاتبة العبد على عتقه (`quranic:root_001283:B005/m01`); أداء الحق واستيفاؤه (`quranic:root_001237:B006/m01`); سلف المصاهرة (`quranic:root_000733:B006/m01`); الغانية المستغنية (`quranic:root_001110:B005/m01`); الغنى والتزويج (`quranic:root_001110:B006/m01`); ائتلاف الشمل بعد تفرق (`quranic:root_000819:B002/m01`); المراضاة والتراضي رضا متبادل (`quranic:root_000569:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ)","synthesis":"Emancipation contract and paid right provide a legal template; intermarried in-laws, the self-sufficient married woman, marriage as protection from need, reunited household, and mutual satisfaction provide the social result. Settlement is therefore also the making or unmaking of durable relations.","ayah_refs":["69:19","69:25","69:27","69:24","69:28","69:21"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: The garden lowers its harvest to the eater","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation joins sheltering growth, vertical abundance, ripened fruit, timely cutting, and bodily access.","parent_surprising_reach":"Elevation is reversed into service: the high place does not distance its produce but bends it close, while past action appears as seed, advance, and first-flowing yield.","key":"B","name":"Ripening, cutting, and gathered cluster","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Fruit reaches its time, is cut from its stem, and remains as an accessible cluster or usable remainder.","active_motifs":"أخذ الشيء بقطعه من أصله (`quranic:root_001241:B001/m01`); الثمر المقطوف والعنقود الداني (`quranic:root_001241:B002/m01`); دنو القِطاف وحلول وقته (`quranic:root_001241:B003/m01`); ما يسقط من القِطاف (`quranic:root_001241:B004/m01`); نبات رخص عريض الورق (`quranic:root_001241:B008/m01`); غلة الشجر والزرع (`quranic:root_000043:B002/m01`); إدراك الثمر وأخذ الطعم (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); إطعام الغصن وقبول الوصل (`quranic:root_000934:B010/m01`); خروج النماء والنتاج (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); السلافة والخلاصة الأولى (`quranic:root_000733:B002/m01`); أكل القضى من عجم الزبيب (`quranic:root_001237:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ)","synthesis":"Cut fruit, the cluster itself, harvest time, dropped gleanings, edible leafy plant, crop yield, ripened produce, accepted graft, emerging return, first pressed liquid, and eaten raisin pits trace a harvest from plant to gathered food and residue. Cutting here realizes maturity rather than destruction.","ayah_refs":["69:23","69:24","69:37","69:34","69:36","69:19","69:25","69:27"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: The garden lowers its harvest to the eater","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation joins sheltering growth, vertical abundance, ripened fruit, timely cutting, and bodily access.","parent_surprising_reach":"Elevation is reversed into service: the high place does not distance its produce but bends it close, while past action appears as seed, advance, and first-flowing yield.","key":"D","name":"Past advance becomes present yield","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Earlier action is stored like seed, capital, or first provision and returns as consumable produce.","active_motifs":"التقدم والسبق (`quranic:root_000733:B001/m01`); السلفة قبل الطعام (`quranic:root_000733:B003/m01`); المال المقدم (`quranic:root_000733:B005/m01`); الأرض المسلوفة (`quranic:root_000733:B010/m01`); خروج النماء والنتاج (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); غلة الشجر والزرع (`quranic:root_000043:B002/m01`); النصيب والرزق المطعوم (`quranic:root_000043:B003/m01`); رزق ومعاش وحسن حال (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`); إدراك الثمر وأخذ الطعم (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); مضي الزمن وذهاب القرن (`quranic:root_000436:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ)","synthesis":"What went before includes prior deed, advance meal, invested capital, and leveled seedbed. Its return appears as crop, allotted food, livelihood, and ripeness; elapsed generations give the causal sequence temporal depth. Reward is thus materialized as a yield whose conditions were laid down earlier.","ayah_refs":["69:24","69:19","69:25","69:37","69:34","69:36"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Handedness organizes enclosure and omen","parent_semantic_invariant":"Right and left are not mere coordinates: they distribute blessing, force, enclosure, social cohesion, cold, and ominous rank.","parent_surprising_reach":"A side becomes a garment, wind, covered weapon, herd-binding device, and social 'gatheredness,' giving handed judgment a full material frame.","key":"A","name":"Right side, oath, blessing, and effective force","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"The right hand receives, pledges, and acts from a culturally favored side.","active_motifs":"اليمن والبركة (`quranic:root_001698:B001/m01`); اليد اليمنى والجهة اليمنى (`quranic:root_001698:B002/m01`); يمين الحلف (`quranic:root_001698:B003/m01`); يمين القوة والحق (`quranic:root_001698:B004/m01`); اليمن البلد والانتساب (`quranic:root_001698:B005/m01`); التيمن الموت (`quranic:root_001698:B007/m01`); الإيتاء والإعطاء (`quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`); حوز الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ي م ن`: 69:19 (يَمِينِ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:30 (خُذُ)","synthesis":"Blessing, right hand, oath, force, geographic rightwardness, and mortal right-side posture gather around receiving and taking. Giving and possession complete the hand-to-hand transfer; the favored side is simultaneously bodily, juridical, and agentive.","ayah_refs":["69:19","69:25","69:30"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Handedness organizes enclosure and omen","parent_semantic_invariant":"Right and left are not mere coordinates: they distribute blessing, force, enclosure, social cohesion, cold, and ominous rank.","parent_surprising_reach":"A side becomes a garment, wind, covered weapon, herd-binding device, and social 'gatheredness,' giving handed judgment a full material frame.","key":"B","name":"Left side, cold current, and failed lot","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"The opposite hand marks spatial contrast and carries associations of cold exposure and unfavorable assignment.","active_motifs":"جهة الشمال خلاف اليمين (`quranic:root_000819:B006/m01`); ريح الشمال وما تبرده (`quranic:root_000819:B007/m01`); الشمول خمرا باردة أو مغطية للعقل (`quranic:root_000819:B009/m01`); الشمال موضع شؤم أو خساسة (`quranic:root_000819:B013/m01`); اليد اليمنى والجهة اليمنى (`quranic:root_001698:B002/m01`); حمحمة الفرس (`quranic:root_000001:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ي م ن`: 69:19 (يَمِينِ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ)","synthesis":"Left hand and north wind establish side and temperature; cold wine overlays the mind, ominous bird or left-hand lot marks failed fortune, and an animal's low vocalization supplies the tense soundscape. The contrast is not a claim that every left sense is punitive, but a local convergence around adverse allocation.","ayah_refs":["69:25","69:19","69:35"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Handedness organizes enclosure and omen","parent_semantic_invariant":"Right and left are not mere coordinates: they distribute blessing, force, enclosure, social cohesion, cold, and ominous rank.","parent_surprising_reach":"A side becomes a garment, wind, covered weapon, herd-binding device, and social 'gatheredness,' giving handed judgment a full material frame.","key":"C","name":"Wrapping, encompassing, and concealed load","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A side-cloth closes around body or object and may hide what is being carried.","active_motifs":"إحاطة واشتمال من الجوانب (`quranic:root_000819:B001/m01`); لف الثوب واشتماله (`quranic:root_000819:B003/m01`); شد وعاء يشتمل على الحيوان أو النخلة (`quranic:root_000819:B004/m01`); حمل مستور في الاشتمال (`quranic:root_000819:B005/m01`); الستر والاستتار (`quranic:root_000266:B001/m01`); الجُنّة الواقية (`quranic:root_000266:B008/m01`); غلالة بين الثياب (`quranic:root_001102:B007/m01`); القطيفة ذات الخمل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_001241:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ)","synthesis":"Encompassing, cloak, tied udder or fruit cover, hidden short sword or stolen animal, generic concealment, shield, undergarment, and plush covering make sidedness into an enclosing textile operation. What the hand receives may be displayed, but what the garment holds can remain unseen.","ayah_refs":["69:25","69:22","69:30","69:23"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Handedness organizes enclosure and omen","parent_semantic_invariant":"Right and left are not mere coordinates: they distribute blessing, force, enclosure, social cohesion, cold, and ominous rank.","parent_surprising_reach":"A side becomes a garment, wind, covered weapon, herd-binding device, and social 'gatheredness,' giving handed judgment a full material frame.","key":"D","name":"Reunited group and inherited disposition","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Scattered persons are gathered into one social whole with recognizable habits and rank.","active_motifs":"ائتلاف الشمل بعد تفرق (`quranic:root_000819:B002/m01`); شمائل الإنسان وخلائقه (`quranic:root_000819:B008/m01`); الشمال موضع شؤم أو خساسة (`quranic:root_000819:B013/m01`); سواد الناس وجماعتهم (`quranic:root_000266:B013/m01`); المراضاة والتراضي رضا متبادل (`quranic:root_000569:B003/m01`); الرضي صفة للمطيع أو المحب أو الضامن (`quranic:root_000569:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ)","synthesis":"Reunited company, personal dispositions, unfavorable status, mass of people, mutual approval, and the obedient or guaranteeing associate describe the social 'side' to which one belongs. Cohesion is ethical as well as numerical.","ayah_refs":["69:25","69:22","69:21"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Seizure becomes linked confinement","parent_semantic_invariant":"Punitive control proceeds from taking the body to fixing a collar, joining links, threading the prisoner through, and measuring the completed restraint.","parent_surprising_reach":"The mechanism reaches wrestling grip, amulet-like arrest, bridle, sewing thread, linked lightning, arm-span, chokehold, and the amount of force a body can bear.","key":"B","name":"Collar, hidden insertion, and fixed restraint","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A device enters around or through the body and remains fixed in its interstices.","active_motifs":"دخول ثابت في الخلال (`quranic:root_001102:B001/m01`); طوق يغل العضو (`quranic:root_001102:B006/m01`); إدخال الحبل في العنق (`quranic:root_000783:B008/m01`); الأخذ بالذراع وتذريعه (`quranic:root_000512:B007/m01`); إسكان الذبيحة بالسكين (`quranic:root_000726:B007/m01`); شد وعاء يشتمل على الحيوان أو النخلة (`quranic:root_000819:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ)","synthesis":"Penetration between parts, iron collar, rope through neck, arm restraint, slaughtering blade that stills movement, and tied animal-cover all share the operation of fastening a living body. The punishment intensifies familiar husbandry and tool functions into immobilization.","ayah_refs":["69:30","69:24","69:32","69:34","69:25"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Seizure becomes linked confinement","parent_semantic_invariant":"Punitive control proceeds from taking the body to fixing a collar, joining links, threading the prisoner through, and measuring the completed restraint.","parent_surprising_reach":"The mechanism reaches wrestling grip, amulet-like arrest, bridle, sewing thread, linked lightning, arm-span, chokehold, and the amount of force a body can bear.","key":"C","name":"Threading through linked material","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A line of connected parts receives another body or material through its continuous path.","active_motifs":"اتصال الحلقات والأجزاء (`quranic:root_000731:B002/m01`); امتداد ملتف متعاقب (`quranic:root_000731:B003/m01`); تردد واضطراب متتابع (`quranic:root_000731:B004/m01`); النفاذ في الطريق (`quranic:root_000735:B001/m01`); إدخال الشيء في الشيء (`quranic:root_000735:B002/m01`); الخيط الذي يخاط به (`quranic:root_000735:B004/m01`); طرة ممتدة في ناحية الثوب (`quranic:root_000735:B005/m01`); دخول ثابت في الخلال (`quranic:root_001102:B001/m01`); سير أو رسالة تتخلل البلاد (`quranic:root_001102:B009/m01`); ضم شيء إلى شيء (`quranic:root_001283:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`س ل س ل`: 69:32 (سِلْسِلَةٍ); `س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ)","synthesis":"Iron links, a winding chain-like extension, repeated disturbance, road penetration, insertion, sewing thread, extended selvedge, deep entry, traveling message, and joined material all define serial continuity. 'Insert him' is thereby rendered as threading a body through a made line.","ayah_refs":["69:32","69:30","69:19","69:25"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Faith is tested as trust and provision","parent_semantic_invariant":"Belief is not isolated assent; it creates security, worshipful orientation, and active responsibility for another person's food and dwelling.","parent_surprising_reach":"Faith's lexical image is a calm heart holding a trust, while neglect of the poor is rendered as refusal to set desire, household, land, and food in motion toward them.","key":"D","name":"Urging, reciprocal encouragement, and increased desire","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Speech and inward appetite are activated so that provision moves toward one who lacks it.","active_motifs":"الحَضّ على الشيء (`quranic:root_000334:B001/m01`); استزادة النفس (`quranic:root_000334:B004/m01`); إطعام الغير وطلب الطعام (`quranic:root_000934:B002/m01`); الهنء عطاء وكفاية (`quranic:root_001604:B002/m01`); العناية الصادقة بالشيء (`quranic:root_001272:B015/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ح ض ض`: 69:34 (يَحُضُّ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ)","synthesis":"Exhortation can be mutual, and the self can be pressed to seek more on another's behalf. Feeding, adequate gift, and sincere care specify the intended outcome; the failure in the surface scene is a failure to initiate this social motion.","ayah_refs":["69:34","69:36","69:24","69:19","69:25"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Faith is tested as trust and provision","parent_semantic_invariant":"Belief is not isolated assent; it creates security, worshipful orientation, and active responsibility for another person's food and dwelling.","parent_surprising_reach":"Faith's lexical image is a calm heart holding a trust, while neglect of the poor is rendered as refusal to set desire, household, land, and food in motion toward them.","key":"E","name":"Poor person, household, and stabilizing food","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Food and shelter arrest involuntary motion by giving a vulnerable person a place and means to remain.","active_motifs":"ذهاب الحركة (`quranic:root_000726:B001/m01`); استيطان المنزل (`quranic:root_000726:B002/m01`); أهل الدار (`quranic:root_000726:B003/m01`); مأنس السكون (`quranic:root_000726:B004/m01`); ذل المسكنة (`quranic:root_000726:B006/m01`); موضع الاستقرار (`quranic:root_000726:B009/m01`); قوت يثبت المقام (`quranic:root_000726:B010/m01`); رزق ومعاش وحسن حال (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`); ائتلاف الشمل بعد تفرق (`quranic:root_000819:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ)","synthesis":"Stillness, dwelling, household, familiar comfort, poverty, stable place, sustaining fodder, livelihood, and gathered company make provision a stabilizing system. The poor person is not only hungry but socially and spatially unanchored; food belongs with household continuity.","ayah_refs":["69:34","69:36","69:25"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Negation strips companion, place, and release","parent_semantic_invariant":"The condemned state is formed by successive absences: no effective wealth, no authority, no close companion, no ordinary food, and no open path away.","parent_surprising_reach":"Lexical absence expands into empty house, elapsed generation, divorce, unguarded object, stationary homebody, and grammatical exclusion.","key":"C","name":"Release, divorce, and abandoned relation","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A bond is opened so each party or dependent is left to a separate course.","active_motifs":"التخلية والإرسال (`quranic:root_000436:B008/m01`); الخلو من الزوج أو الولد (`quranic:root_000436:B009/m01`); الخلية من الإبل (`quranic:root_000436:B010/m01`); المخالاة في المفارقة والمصارعة (`quranic:root_000436:B014/m01`); السخرية أو المخادعة في الخلو (`quranic:root_000436:B015/m01`); ائتلاف الشمل بعد تفرق (`quranic:root_000819:B002/m01`); المراضاة والتراضي رضا متبادل (`quranic:root_000569:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ)","synthesis":"Release, divorced or childless status, a dam separated from her young, broken truce, mutual exposure in wrestling, deception in private, scattered company, and lost mutual satisfaction describe relational unbinding. Freedom and abandonment share the same opening operation but differ in who benefits.","ayah_refs":["69:24","69:25","69:21"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Hidden life proceeds toward birth","parent_semantic_invariant":"Generation moves through concealed interior, sexual readiness, conception, gestation, visible swelling, and the appointed time of emergence.","parent_surprising_reach":"The process connects womb, menstrual interval, animal heat, hoped-for child, near delivery, fruit ripening, grafting, and failed fertility.","key":"A","name":"Womb, concealed fetus, and gathered bodily matter","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An interior chamber gathers blood or seed and shelters developing life.","active_motifs":"الجنين المستور في البطن (`quranic:root_000266:B007/m01`); الجنان المستور في الصدر (`quranic:root_000266:B010/m01`); الجناجن عظام الصدر (`quranic:root_000266:B016/m01`); قرء الحيض والطهر (`quranic:root_001210:B003/m01`); رحم يجمع حملا (`quranic:root_001210:B004/m01`); وقت الشيء ودورانه (`quranic:root_001211:B002/m01`); اجتماع الرحم وحمله (`quranic:root_001211:B003/m01`); لقاح جزئي أو حمل الناقة (`quranic:root_000819:B012/m01`); دخول ثابت في الخلال (`quranic:root_001102:B001/m01`); إمساك للاستبراء أو حبس (`quranic:root_001210:B010/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ)","synthesis":"Fetus, inward heart-chamber, chest bones, menstrual or pure interval, womb-gathered pregnancy, partial conception, penetration, and detention for pregnancy verification form a concealed reproductive assembly. The body is both container and timed process.","ayah_refs":["69:22","69:19","69:25","69:30"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Hidden life proceeds toward birth","parent_semantic_invariant":"Generation moves through concealed interior, sexual readiness, conception, gestation, visible swelling, and the appointed time of emergence.","parent_surprising_reach":"The process connects womb, menstrual interval, animal heat, hoped-for child, near delivery, fruit ripening, grafting, and failed fertility.","key":"B","name":"Readiness, mating, and uncertain fertility","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Female readiness and male performance are tested by whether conception actually follows.","active_motifs":"استئتاء الناقة (`quranic:root_000009:B012/m01`); قرء الناقة في الضبعة والوداق (`quranic:root_001210:B009/m01`); سرعة اللقاح (`quranic:root_001372:B003/m01`); فحل يكثر الضراب ولا يلقح (`quranic:root_001087:B003/m01`); لقاح جزئي أو حمل الناقة (`quranic:root_000819:B012/m01`); امرأة يرجى ولدها (`quranic:root_000969:B009/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ل ق ي`: 69:20 (مُلَٰقٍ); `غ س ل`: 69:36 (غِسْلِينٍ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن)","synthesis":"Mare in heat, camel mating-readiness, rapid conception, a frequently mating but infertile male, partial insemination, and a woman from whom children are hoped describe the uncertain transition from coupling to generation.","ayah_refs":["69:19","69:25","69:20","69:36"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Hidden life proceeds toward birth","parent_semantic_invariant":"Generation moves through concealed interior, sexual readiness, conception, gestation, visible swelling, and the appointed time of emergence.","parent_surprising_reach":"The process connects womb, menstrual interval, animal heat, hoped-for child, near delivery, fruit ripening, grafting, and failed fertility.","key":"C","name":"Approaching birth, first growth, and ripened issue","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A hidden process becomes externally legible as its time and product approach.","active_motifs":"قرب النتاج (`quranic:root_000493:B004/m01`); جن الشيء في بدايته (`quranic:root_000266:B014/m01`); دنو القِطاف وحلول وقته (`quranic:root_001241:B003/m01`); إدراك الثمر وأخذ الطعم (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); إطعام الغصن وقبول الوصل (`quranic:root_000934:B010/m01`); خروج النماء والنتاج (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); هناءة الماشية من النبت (`quranic:root_001604:B003/m01`); تتابع الخلق (`quranic:root_000934:B014/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`د ن و`: 69:23 (دَانِيَةٌ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا)","synthesis":"Near delivery, the first stage of youth, harvest time, fruit taking flavor, successful graft, emerging produce, an animal's share of fresh growth, and successive formation all express maturation becoming available at the boundary.","ayah_refs":["69:23","69:22","69:34","69:36","69:19","69:25","69:24"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Speech moves between utterance, belief, and verdict","parent_semantic_invariant":"A statement can be voiced, circulated, negotiated, held inwardly, treated as a proposition, or imposed as judgment.","parent_surprising_reach":"Speech becomes tongue, reputation, bargaining, private thought, logical definition, song, excessive talk, and a sharp instrument of authority.","key":"A","name":"Voiced statement and circulating talk","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A speaker externalizes words that pass among people as report or negotiation.","active_motifs":"إخراج القول بالنطق (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); اللسان آلة القول (`quranic:root_001272:B002/m01`); كثرة القول في صاحبه (`quranic:root_001272:B003/m01`); القول الفاشي بين الناس (`quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`); المقاولة في الأمر (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`); قراءة وتلاوة وإقراء (`quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`); الغِناء والصوت (`quranic:root_001110:B003/m01`); إفراط اللسان والكلام (`quranic:root_000512:B011/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا)","synthesis":"Utterance, tongue, fluent speaker, circulating report, mutual discussion, recitation, song, and excessive speech form the audible social life of the statement. Reading the record is therefore one regulated form within a wider field of speech circulation.","ayah_refs":["69:19","69:25","69:28","69:32"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Speech moves between utterance, belief, and verdict","parent_semantic_invariant":"A statement can be voiced, circulated, negotiated, held inwardly, treated as a proposition, or imposed as judgment.","parent_surprising_reach":"Speech becomes tongue, reputation, bargaining, private thought, logical definition, song, excessive talk, and a sharp instrument of authority.","key":"B","name":"Fabricated attribution, inward statement, and proposition","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Speech may be falsely assigned, silently held, adopted as belief, or abstracted into a definition.","active_motifs":"قول ما لم يكن أو نسبته (`quranic:root_001272:B005/m01`); اجترار القول إلى النفس (`quranic:root_001272:B006/m01`); قول يجري مجرى الظن (`quranic:root_001272:B011/m01`); قول في النفس لم يظهر (`quranic:root_001272:B012/m01`); القول اعتقاد ومذهب (`quranic:root_001272:B013/m01`); قول الشيء دلالته (`quranic:root_001272:B014/m01`); قول الشيء حده (`quranic:root_001272:B016/m01`); شك وتوهم (`quranic:root_000969:B003/m01`); تهمة وظنة (`quranic:root_000969:B004/m01`); سوء الظن بالناس (`quranic:root_000969:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن)","synthesis":"Putting words in another's mouth, appropriating a saying, using speech as conjecture, keeping it inward, holding a doctrine, letting a thing 'say' by sign, and defining its essence move speech away from simple sound. Doubt, accusation, and habitual suspicion expose the ethical risk in that expansion.","ayah_refs":["69:19","69:25","69:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Speech moves between utterance, belief, and verdict","parent_semantic_invariant":"A statement can be voiced, circulated, negotiated, held inwardly, treated as a proposition, or imposed as judgment.","parent_surprising_reach":"Speech becomes tongue, reputation, bargaining, private thought, logical definition, song, excessive talk, and a sharp instrument of authority.","key":"D","name":"Ruling speech and the force of proof","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A claim becomes operative when backed by proof, office, and a conclusive decision.","active_motifs":"السلطان حجة قاهرة (`quranic:root_000732:B002/m01`); السلطان قدرة وولاية (`quranic:root_000732:B003/m01`); لسان سليط حاد صخاب (`quranic:root_000732:B004/m01`); الحكم والأمر القاطع (`quranic:root_001237:B001/m01`); الإبلاغ والوصية القاطعة (`quranic:root_001237:B002/m01`); القيل صاحب القول النافذ (`quranic:root_001272:B004/m01`); اقتالة الحكم على غيره (`quranic:root_001272:B010/m01`); إثبات يوجب حكما أو قدرا (`quranic:root_001283:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`س ل ط`: 69:29 (سُلْطَٰنِ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ)","synthesis":"Prevailing proof, delegated authority, sharp tongue, adjudication, formal notice, authoritative speaker, imposed judgment, and written decree join discourse to enforcement. The verdict is speech whose social conditions make it binding.","ayah_refs":["69:29","69:27","69:19","69:25"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Speech moves between utterance, belief, and verdict","parent_semantic_invariant":"A statement can be voiced, circulated, negotiated, held inwardly, treated as a proposition, or imposed as judgment.","parent_surprising_reach":"Speech becomes tongue, reputation, bargaining, private thought, logical definition, song, excessive talk, and a sharp instrument of authority.","key":"F","name":"Claim of unconsumed drink and contested conduct","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A speaker imputes an act to another, who must distinguish actual conduct from verbal accusation.","active_motifs":"دعوى ما لم يشرب (`quranic:root_000783:B010/m01`); قول ما لم يكن أو نسبته (`quranic:root_001272:B005/m01`); تهمة وظنة (`quranic:root_000969:B004/m01`); التحسب والاستخبار (`quranic:root_000318:B010/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ)","synthesis":"The claim that another drank what they did not, fabricated attribution, accusation, and inquiry into the report create a compact dispute over conduct and evidence.","ayah_refs":["69:24","69:19","69:25","69:20","69:26"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Cloth is wrapped, lined, threaded, and worn","parent_semantic_invariant":"Textile construction proceeds from joined material through inner lining, thread-path, cut edge, wrapping, and protective or deceptive surface.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garment making reaches waterskin stitching, shoe lining, plush bedding, hidden sword, under-armor layer, leather residue, and cloth whose colors misrepresent its state.","key":"A","name":"Wrapping, underlayer, and hidden load","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Cloth encloses the body and creates an inner layer in which another object may be concealed.","active_motifs":"لف الثوب واشتماله (`quranic:root_000819:B003/m01`); حمل مستور في الاشتمال (`quranic:root_000819:B005/m01`); غلالة بين الثياب (`quranic:root_001102:B007/m01`); السلفة بطانة الخف (`quranic:root_000733:B013/m01`); القطيفة ذات الخمل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_001241:B007/m01`); الستر والاستتار (`quranic:root_000266:B001/m01`); الجُنّة الواقية (`quranic:root_000266:B008/m01`); مواراة الميت (`quranic:root_000266:B009/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ)","synthesis":"Cloak, object hidden under the cloak, undergarment, shoe lining, plush cover, generic concealment, shield, and burial covering organize textile by layers. Protection and concealment are neighboring but distinct uses of the same enclosing surface.","ayah_refs":["69:25","69:30","69:24","69:23","69:22"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Cloth is wrapped, lined, threaded, and worn","parent_semantic_invariant":"Textile construction proceeds from joined material through inner lining, thread-path, cut edge, wrapping, and protective or deceptive surface.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garment making reaches waterskin stitching, shoe lining, plush bedding, hidden sword, under-armor layer, leather residue, and cloth whose colors misrepresent its state.","key":"B","name":"Thread, joined leather, and extended edge","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Thread passes through joined material and continues as a stable seam or border.","active_motifs":"إدخال الشيء في الشيء (`quranic:root_000735:B002/m01`); الخيط الذي يخاط به (`quranic:root_000735:B004/m01`); طرة ممتدة في ناحية الثوب (`quranic:root_000735:B005/m01`); ضم شيء إلى شيء (`quranic:root_001283:B001/m01`); دخول ثابت في الخلال (`quranic:root_001102:B001/m01`); بلى السقاء لطول الترك (`quranic:root_001237:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ); `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ)","synthesis":"Insertion, sewing thread, long selvedge, joined leather, penetration between parts, and decayed waterskin form a complete seam-making scene. A worn container fails where the join no longer holds.","ayah_refs":["69:32","69:19","69:25","69:30","69:27"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Cloth is wrapped, lined, threaded, and worn","parent_semantic_invariant":"Textile construction proceeds from joined material through inner lining, thread-path, cut edge, wrapping, and protective or deceptive surface.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garment making reaches waterskin stitching, shoe lining, plush bedding, hidden sword, under-armor layer, leather residue, and cloth whose colors misrepresent its state.","key":"C","name":"Cut cloth, polished pattern, and misleading color","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A surface is cut and finished into a visible textile whose appearance may or may not disclose its substance.","active_motifs":"لف الثوب واشتماله (`quranic:root_000819:B003/m01`); القطيفة ذات الخمل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_001241:B007/m01`); بقية لحم في الإهاب (`quranic:root_001102:B012/m01`); لون الأحسب والأحسبية (`quranic:root_000318:B009/m01`); عود القال لضرب القلة (`quranic:root_001272:B008/m01`); طرة ممتدة في ناحية الثوب (`quranic:root_000735:B005/m01`); مخالطة اللون والقلب (`quranic:root_000783:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ)","synthesis":"Wrapped cloth, pile fabric, flesh left on a hide, mottled coloring, color absorbed into material or heart, a wooden bat, and an extended textile edge place finish and appearance beside imperfect preparation. The scene pressures the book's legibility by asking whether a surface is well made or merely visually persuasive.","ayah_refs":["69:25","69:23","69:30","69:20","69:26","69:19","69:32","69:24"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Cloth is wrapped, lined, threaded, and worn","parent_semantic_invariant":"Textile construction proceeds from joined material through inner lining, thread-path, cut edge, wrapping, and protective or deceptive surface.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garment making reaches waterskin stitching, shoe lining, plush bedding, hidden sword, under-armor layer, leather residue, and cloth whose colors misrepresent its state.","key":"D","name":"Protective covering and bodily stilling","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A made covering shields a vulnerable body or arrests its movement.","active_motifs":"الجُنّة الواقية (`quranic:root_000266:B008/m01`); مواراة الميت (`quranic:root_000266:B009/m01`); إسكان الذبيحة بالسكين (`quranic:root_000726:B007/m01`); شد وعاء يشتمل على الحيوان أو النخلة (`quranic:root_000819:B004/m01`); طوق يغل العضو (`quranic:root_001102:B006/m01`); غلالة بين الثياب (`quranic:root_001102:B007/m01`); الأخذ بالذراع وتذريعه (`quranic:root_000512:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا)","synthesis":"Shield, burial cover, knife, tied udder cover, iron collar, under-armor layer, and arm-restraint all use material at the body boundary. ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
